  My 1ife traces back to Kanagawa Prefecture of Japan,October 27, 1944,the year that saw the coming
end of World War II.   In the year,witnesses of flying object called   "Foo-Fighters" were reported over
European Continent and Pacific Ocean.    In August of t.hat year, my mother also saw a flashing object
appear against the mount.ain and disappear into tho town.


  I was a 10-year-old boy when I first knew about the Unidentified Flying Object (U F O)through my
classmate's story about a flying disk that flew from the sky over to the telephone service pole.


  I joined UFO research organization in 1%0. Through the magazines released by the organization,
came to know worldwide reports of witnesses and a variety of UFO researches. I began to witness var­
ious kinds of UFO: some were disks with dome and some were salient shaped. Shining flying objects in
a group flew zigzag toward Jupiter and Saturn. Such witnesses counted over 100. I took consecutive
shots of a UFO flying to disappear into the town. Also I succeeded three limes in recording UFO on
video tape. Through these experience, I came to believe in the existence of extraordinary flying ob­
jects and unprecedented phenomenon that show somewhere in the sky,which can not go with traditional
explanation.


  Treasurer is my teachers,    who are pioneers in UFOLCX:iY in Japan and are well known figures in the
world. They taught me many things: �at are the features of UFO? How to produce statistics and to                                                       .1985.if-4 fj 168-f"1.£12�3()5J\.ADDIS ABABA jpf.:>

And the mo5t important que5tion is where and for what purpose does UFO come?

  I am A supporter of a belief that UFO comes a long way to Earth from the edge of the universe.

There are reasons for t.he belief. One of my seniors disclosed his own experience to me : he found
himself aboard a huge cigar-shaped apacecraft hovering in the air and got a notice of coming changes

  We named those spacecrafts "Cosmic Union",which we believe have paid repeated visits to the earth
since the ancient time back to thousands of years. Flying objects appear so real on the Old Testament
as Pillar of fire,Pillar of cloud,and Chariot of Fire and Horses of Fire.      "('JOd "and   "angel "could
be teachers from the universe. We can find description of multiple sun-like object s in Ancient Egypt
      y
Histor ,which might be UFOs. Jesus Christ went up to heaven on a cloud carpet. Where is the heaven?
He ate fish and went back as a living thing like us human.      So it is reasonable to think that He
dwells in the air.An ancient tale of North America describes the carriage that He took to the heaven
as a snake with wings.      As there were no machines like airplane, the carriage found expression in
animals, birds, the sun, and stars. Now we have many helpful expressions such as plate and cigar.
Modern people never mi51.mderstand airplane and spacecraft.


  Witness reports are key for the UFOLOGY. Therefore re��rts should be free from lies,modifications,
and imagination, and should be just the way it is. ·rnerefore it is significant to fo5ter our succes­
sors that are willing to express what they witnessed as they were. Our earth is open to the univers.
  It is open to any phenomenon and disasters.    In China, a nK>torman put on sudden brake on the train
51Jrprised by an appearance of a UFO just before a big earthquake, and eventually could avoide a tra­
ffic catastrophe. Such report of UFO at disastered site is meaningful. Miracle may happen in China
again, and in Japan too. Maybe simultaneously worldwide. We are starved for honest witness reports.


  In near future, when UFO may make a memorable, friendly landing on the earth and crew of UFO may
inspire know-how to solve problems that the earth will be struggling with,the receiver must be our
peaceful! off5�rings. Along with study of UFO and UFO phenomenon, we have to make every effort to
produce peace on the earth as a citizen of the universe by learning the way of THEM in keeping peace
there. This book is for our fellow on earth who accept the UFO in the same altitude as mine.

  A    sign      of    civilization?




 A sign of civilization? The Viking 1 orbiter took this picture of mesalike landforms in
 the Elysium region of Mars on July 25,1976. Shadowing on one of the mesas(above center)
 has given it the appearance of a human head. The feature, about 1,5km across, was nick­
 named ill "The Face of Mars. "

 "SPACE SHIPS" CAUSE SENSATION by Walt Rogal, New Mexico Newspaper staff writer:
For the third consecutive day flying saucer have been reported over Farmington.
And on each of the three days their arrival was reported between 11 A.M. and noon.
